- **Package map:** `internal/worker/routing.go` is the S08.8 selection engine (`Router`: selector match → tie-break seam → duty-map resolution under subscription coverage; `DutyMap`/`Seat`, `Coverage`, `TieBreaker` + `PressureReader` seams, `Decision`, gap bookkeeping) with `search.go` + migration **0006** (`worker_search` FTS5 over delegation descriptions — refreshed in-tx by the two `active_version` writers, Approve/Repoint; status is ALWAYS filtered from `worker_templates`, never trusted to the index). `internal/intake/route.go` is the seam intake consumes (`intake.Router`, `RouteBlock` on the approval card + state, `RouteOverride` on the answer). `internal/stage/router.go` bridges them and owns event emission; `internal/stage/helpers.go` is the S04 spawn/helper slice. Composition: the shell wires `stage.Config.Workers` (+ `RoutePressure` = the S10.4 gauge adapted); `worker` never imports `metering`/`intake`/`stage` — seams only.
- **Selection discipline (S08.8):** deterministic, free-tier, and dollar-free by construction — no price/cost figure exists among the router's inputs; among flat-rate lanes the ordering input is consumption pressure (D5), moot-but-wired at one lane. Filters exactly per step 1: status=active, kind, domain, required grants ⊆ granted, confinement equal-or-tighter (S11 `LadderRank`, exported for this comparison). Candidate order: score desc → name → id (stable); tie-break is the S12 seam, and its ABSENCE at v0 degrades to that deterministic order **with the absence recorded in the plain reason** (absent duties degrade, never faked). `routing.decided` is the settled event name (R12 §4.1) — emitted per run at execute dispatch and per helper spawn; recording failure fails the dispatch (silent routing is banned, 14.3). `worker.compiled` (provisional naming, standing §7/§8 note) records each per-spawn ConfigHash on the run (S08.3 "recorded on the run").
- **The v0 duty map is code DATA, gate-flagged:** `worker.DefaultDutyMap()` — execution/planning/judge seats all `claude-haiku-4-5` on the anthropic lane, 200k window riding the seat row (where the B2-4 `stage.DefaultModel` + window constants retired). Readings, section-cited: (1) the demonstrated "best result per unit of allowance" posture (S06.10) — the model the B1-1 smoke, B1-4 battery, and ratified B2 demo ran every seat on; (2) recording the de-facto judge model as the map default is NOT a P-T06-5 version bump (no model moved); any FUTURE seat retarget is a bump gated on a golden-set re-run (§15); (3) the utility seat is deliberately ABSENT (S06.10 pins it local; S12 lands B4) — utility/local-preferring duties ride the execution seat with the degradation in the reason; (4) no S18 key covers the map (the §7/§9/§11 precedent; the standing settings-tab directive is the resolution path). Subscription coverage binds every choice: `Coverage.MeteredAllowed` stays nil in production (metered-exception list EMPTY, G1 P7) and the metered branch structurally refuses — pinned by test.
- **No-fit & gap discipline (S08.8/S08.6):** the two-stage card rides the Stage-4 approval card (stage 1 = the S06 restatement on the same surface; stage 2 = the routing block offering generalist-default (degraded-marked from the `domains` row; an ABSENT row is degraded — 2.1 honesty) / compose-when-earned / model-gap advice). Gap records write through the B3-2 `RecordGap` — **recurrence means DISTINCT tasks**: re-routing the same task (card rebuilds, re-plans) re-reads the standing record, never increments. Gap advice is config-derived at v0 and says so (observed per-account model lists are P-T17-3/S03.6 watch machinery, B5). Compose-when-earned at v0 surfaces the earned state; acting on it is the human-authoring path (`CreateDraft` + full battery) — the S08.6 composer one-shot GENERATION session is deliberately unbuilt (no packet has carried it; nothing fakes it).
- **Override discipline (S08.8):** re-route/pin is an approval-answer field (`Answer.Route`), valid with Approve/Re-plan/Re-interview, validated against the card's offered candidate set (an override can never widen beyond what selection admitted), recorded as a human ledger decision with its actor. A PIN survives re-planning recomputes by FILTERING the fresh selection (never skipping it); a pinned worker that no longer fits the re-planned requirements is dropped LOUDLY in the plain reason.
- **S04 slice (B3 queue-header reading):** spawn mechanics, helper topology, and the D6 shape land here; the ≥50-item mass-mechanical fan-out stays parked post-v0 (S19.4). Structural D6: helpers are engine sessions ON the coordinator's run (checkpoints/usage land there — D7; helpers are NOT run rows, so the S02.5 ladder never touches them: death = SALVAGE, a scheduling event), hold no ledger verbs/DB reach (write nothing durable), and the PLATFORM lands the screened report in the coordinator's L0 (per-spawn workspace file `helpers/<spawn>/report.md` [coordinator-draft subpath] + ledger artifact ref + annotations; verbatim — the filed report is the helper's words). No lateral channel exists as API fact; the engine-native `Task*` family in a helper toolset is refused at spawn (the only conceivable lateral/native vector on the lane, S03.5). Depth derives from the in-process spawn-lineage registry (a sub-helper is proposed by its LIVE parent); spawn budget counts durable spawn records per task incl. retries; a retry is a FRESH spawn with the rejection folded into boundaries (fork-don't-poison). The acceptance screen is conformance-ONLY (G1 Def.9/G3 Def.3): sections + ⚙ size (bytes/4 estimate, §17 precedent) + unfinished-marker consistency; boundary respect is structural (toolset/class enforced it); the instruction-pattern screen (worker.ScreenInstructions) annotates report-as-data, never rewords. Refusals are logged evidence naming the failed check; every lifecycle transition is an event; the S04.5 conformance set (depth-3, native/lateral tool, over-budget, mid-run kill → salvage-with-marker, sibling containment, escalation-ask reachability) is a standing battery entry.
- **⚙ consumed here** (dotted key, never hardcoded): 8 of the 9 S04 keys — `orchestration.{depth_cap, max_concurrent_helpers, helper_turns, helper_tokens, spawn_budget, report_tokens, bulk_offload_tokens, helper_retry_limit}`. `orchestration.stagger_identical_prefix` is DELIBERATELY UNCONSUMED (named-seam comment, the B2-4 recitation precedent): staggering applies to simultaneously-launched identical-prefix fan-outs and no simultaneous-launch batch surface exists at v0 — consuming it with nothing to stagger would fake the mechanism. S08's 4 `workers.*` keys stay consumed where B3-2 put them (`gap_proposal_count` re-read through the no-fit path).
- **Event names:** `routing.decided` settled (spec-fixed); `worker.compiled`, `orchestration.{spawn, spawn_refused, helper}` provisional pending the S14 contract (B5) — extending the standing §7/§8/§13–§18 note. The spawn record payload is the full S04.4 field set (OTel-mappable shape).
- **Engine-pin note:** the B3-3 reconfirm ran on installed `claude` 2.1.216 vs lock pin 2.1.215 — reported per §10 (assert behavior, never retarget); the operator S03.3 bump decision is a B3 gate item. Result file: `P3/measurements/2026-07-21-serialize-by-deny-reconfirm-s08.md` (PASS; TBD-BRINGUP(parallel-gate fallback) CLOSED on the S08-designated default worker).

## 20. Recitation-channel conventions (P3-B3-4; S05.3–S05.4, S03.4, Research/18 §7-C1)

- **The split (§7-C1 condition 6):** the PLATFORM computes turns-dueness and authors; the engine-side hook is a delivery valve ONLY. `internal/stage/recite.go` owns the reciter — dueness from persisted run events via the `OnEvent` observer seam (one turn = one per-call usage event, the D7 paid-call granularity; counted per stage session, reset at each authoring); ⚙ `context.recitation_interval_turns` read per session through the registry (0 = off) — the B2-4 "deliberately unconsumed" seam CLOSED. `internal/adapters/claudecli/recite.go` owns the valve (`sinet engine-hook --ctl <dir> --post-tool-use`), compiled by the lowering when `CompiledWorker.Recitation`: PostToolUse matcher = the FULL tool-allowlist alternation (every allowed tool is a delivery boundary; a turn without tool calls delivers at the next one); recitation on a tool-less session refuses at lowering (no boundary could ever fire). **PreToolUse stays single-purpose** (condition 4) — hook.go untouched, byte-identity-pinned by test. **Scope v0 = recitation ONLY** (condition 1): the pending file carries recipient-fixed platform-authored ledger `state`/`next_actions` at a pinned `ledger_version` (`ledger.RecitationText`, deterministic — no clock/env input, so the manifest can re-render and compare); cancel-notice/operator-note delivery is NOT adopted (S00.9 amendment territory); free-form messaging never (D6).
- **Transport = the existing S03.4 airlock** (the §7-C1 coherence correction — NO new socket, credential, or principal): `<ctl>/recite/pending.json` written by the platform via temp-file + atomic rename (a re-authoring atomically supersedes an undelivered pending — newest platform truth wins); the valve claims by rename (exactly one winner under parallel fires, losers see ENOENT and print nothing), retains the claimed copy as `delivered-<ns>.json`, and appends `<ctl>/recite.fires.log` one line per DELIVERY with the content hash RECOMPUTED over the delivered bytes (never trusted from the payload). Quiet inbox = zero stdout = zero injected context (probe P2). Under confinement the ctl dir is the rw exchange whenever gated tools OR recitation need it — still the ONE rw bind (§12); compiled settings stay platform-placed and read-only (P-T09-1/S11.7), re-asserted in the leak suite with the hook added (condition 7).
- **Which sessions recite:** ledger-backed working sessions only — `Assemble && !Clean` with a non-empty toolset. Artifact-only sessions (§14 critique, §15/§16 retry package: input contracts exclude the ledger projection) and clean-context verification (S05.4 exception) never recite. A fresh (non-resume) spawn resets recite state regardless of the Recitation bit (a fresh session starts un-recited; stale state can neither deliver nor double-manifest); a resume keeps an undelivered pending deliverable at its recorded pinned version.
- **Every delivery manifested + journaled, from FIRES only** (condition 3): stage mirrors the fires/pending shapes without importing the substrate (§13 precedent; the mirror is pinned by the e2e — the stage-authored bytes are consumed by the real valve) and appends one `context.manifest` `kind:"recitation"` run event per fire for EVERY session disposition (a delivery into a later-failed session still happened). The manifest path re-renders at the pinned revision and hash-verifies: mismatch/unknown-revision land as entry dispositions (`hash_mismatch`/`unknown_version`), recorded loudly and never dropped — in-sandbox tampering with the engine-writable exchange dir cannot make the trace lie. The crash window between claim and fire-append leaves on-disk delivered-* evidence and NO manifest claim (nothing unevidenced ever claims injection); the next dueness re-authors.
- **Engine facts + C3 rows:** the channel rests on the PASS probe at installed 2.1.216 (`P3/measurements/2026-07-21-posttooluse-additionalcontext-probe.md`; spend $0.027 — consumed by this packet, never re-paid); its four per-pin canary rows are executable as the `SINET_B3_4=1`-gated live test in claudecli (rows 1–2 + row 3's identity subset ride the SHIPPED path; the full payload-set and lifecycle-frame rows re-check via the probe method at each S03.3 bump). C3 conformance rows landed: interaction-boundary crash cases (`internal/adapters/crash_boundary_test.go` — restart-with-pending and crash-after-answer, each reconstructed from DB state alone) and the error-output-never-becomes-stage-output negative row (claudecli), both carrying the dated §3-C3 evidence notes (codor `305670fb554`, 2026-07-20, Agent-SDK path — verify at Sinet's own pin before relying). **C5 (advisory copy-aside context peek) deliberately not built** — discretionary row, no slack spent; drops without loss per Research/18 §7-C5.

## 21. Composer-generation & stage-split conventions (P3-B3-5; S08.6, S09.10, S05.3)

- **Package map:** `internal/worker/composer.go` is the S08.6 composer verb (`Store.Compose`: policy-input assembly → ONE `ComposeEngine.ComposeOnce` → `CreateDraft` with composer provenance → gap `composed`; plus `Gap`/`ListDomains` reads and the `composeReference` renderer) with the playbook seed content in `worker/playbook.go`; `internal/stage/compose.go` is the composition leg (`EngineComposer`, the production `DryEngine` (`EngineDryRun` — the §18 named seam closed), `ensureComposeRun`, `dispatchCompose` on the new `roleCompose`); `internal/stage/split.go` is the S05.3 split executor (`runPlannedStage` sub-loop + `consolidateSplit`); `internal/ledger/substage.go` owns sub-stage identity (`SubStageName`/`PlannedStage`). §19's "the S08.6 composer one-shot GENERATION session is deliberately unbuilt" is CLOSED here.
- **Stage-split execution (S05.3):** the watcher's stage-split proposal is auto-accepted — the `context.overflow` event commits FIRST (fully-logged scheduling), then `budgetWatcher.onStageSplit` requests the S03.1 **pause** verb via the new `adapters.StartRequest.OnSession` control seam (`json:"-"`, the OnEvent posture; the Driver hands the live session out after every successful spawn). The boundary interrupt is the pause verb's existing safe-boundary contract (never mid-tool-call, P-T01-4); a pause that races completion loses and the event stays a proposal record. A split outcome (`SessionResult.Split`: parked, no ask, split requested) **discards the park record** — the successor is a fresh brief from the updated ledger, never a resume (S05.3/G1 D1.1). Only ledger-backed working sessions split (`Assemble && !Clean`, the §20 recitation class): artifact-only/clean input contracts exclude the ledger projection, so no successor brief could carry their state — for them the event remains a proposal record.
- **Split consolidation** (`consolidateSplit`, platform acts — the engine-facing verb TOOL channel remains later wiring, §14): platform ledger decision (author `platform`, citing the overflow event seq) → `Verbs.State` keeps the step `in_progress` and hands it forward by BARE ITEM ID in `state.next_actions` (the `CheckStageClose` handed-forward contract, §13) → the stage-close gate enforced on the ended sub-stage. The successor assembles at the bumped `ledger_version` (proven by manifest assertion in the e2e); the per-run cwd persists across sub-sessions, the per-STAGE work dir does not (fresh ctl dir per sub-session).
- **Overflow scope is the PLANNED stage** (S05.3 "a second overflow within one planned stage"): `runPlannedStage` seeds `PriorOverflows` 0 per plan step and carries it across that step's sub-sessions only — the B2-4 cross-step accumulation is corrected (the `SessionInput.PriorOverflows` doc already said per-planned-stage). First crossing → split (executed); any later crossing in the same planned stage → re-plan proposal, never a second split — the loop is structurally bounded. Worker-backed sub-sessions recompile per sub-stage (`CompileForRun` with the concrete sub-stage in instance refs — fresh every run, S08.3).
- **Sub-stage naming:** `<step>#<k>` (k ≥ 2); `ledger.PlannedStage` strips only a trailing `#<digits>` (conservative), and `intake.PlanSource` injects the PLANNED step's contract for sub-stages (the split never changes what the plan assigned).
- **Composer (S08.6):** `ComposeEngine.ComposeOnce` is the one-shot seam — `Store.Compose` takes exactly one shot; a rejected draft (`ErrComposeRejected`: empty/unparseable/guardrail-field — the S08.2 reject fires at `CreateDraft`) is NEVER regenerated, and the battery runs strictly AFTER composition through the caller (`dispatchCompose`), so the composer structurally cannot see validation results (the banned generate-test-refine loop). The `jsonSession` transport bounce (§15 `jsonRetryLimit`) recovers a malformed ENVELOPE only — documented as not battery-iteration. Inputs by policy, exhaustively: task spec (`intake.CurrentPair` — new exported pre-approval read; D10 untouched, nothing executes from it) + gap record (loaded from the store, required) + playbook (required — absent seam/object refuses loudly, the §14 absent-required-duty discipline) + palette (EMPTY at v0 — no harvest import exists; the slot renders "(none available)") + `composeReference` (schema/lint/ceilings/domains rendered from the SAME authorities the battery enforces — no drift). Provenance: `Composer` = the seat model as a platform fact (never model output), `PlaybookVer` = `<entry>@v<version>`, `EvidenceRef` = `gap:<signature>`, origin `composed`; the gap flips `composed` at draft creation (adoption stays the battery/approval's).
- **Composition run & verb:** `ActionCompose` on the approval card (offered only while `ComposeEarned` && no prior request; ONE composition per task — recurrence earns more through new tasks' gap occurrences) keeps the ask OPEN and records `State.Compose`; `afterIntake` idempotently launches `<task>.compose` (create + Enqueue, interactive class; `ErrExists` + not-new ⇒ already ran). `dispatchCompose`: generation + battery (station 3 via `EngineDryRun` — a stage session running the compiled draft on the ceremony run) + a platform ledger decision recording the outcome; draft-rejection and red batteries COMPLETE the run (the ceremony happened; receipts stand), mechanical failures crash for the ladder. `metering.RunSuffixCompose` → purpose **ceremony** (billed/itemized to the requester, 1.10/3.4). Sample curation = edit/replace on the card driving a fresh `RunBattery` pass (S08.6 sample authorship; no new surface).
- **Playbook governance (S09.10):** content owned by S08 (`worker.ComposerPlaybookSeed`, topic key `worker/composer-playbook`); `memory.EnsureComposerPlaybook` seeds it as a governed house object (fixed id `seed-composer-playbook`, kind `playbook`, file-backed markdown — its consumer is a prompt, not a `Load*` parser; the §17 idempotency/no-resurrection/`ErrNoOperator` discipline) with provenance stating ratification PENDING at the B3 gate; `memory.Store.HouseObject(topicKey)` serves the CURRENT APPROVED version (active house entry by topic key — supersession retires in-tx per §17, so active IS current), read into stage through `Config.ComposerPlaybook` at the composition root (stage never imports memory, §17). `Config.EnginePin` (shell wires `claudecli.Pin`) keys the leg's validation records; **`claudecli.Pin` promoted from the conformance test into the package** (same name, same `TestPinMatchesLock` coupling — the composition root now consumes it as data, S08.1).
- **Clearly-implied readings (section-cited):** (1) the compose verb requires a standing gap record even for explicit requests — S08.8 writes one at every no-fit, and S08.6 lists "the task spec + gap record" as policy inputs. (2) Composer sessions run `Assemble: false`: S08.6's inputs-by-policy REPLACES stage-brief assembly; the enumerated prompt frame plus version-carrying provenance is the injection record. (3) `Decision`/battery separation means a red battery leaves the drafted template in `draft` with its record — exactly the human-authored path's posture (validation asserts structure, never quality). (4) The one-per-task rule realizes S08.6's economics (composition never rides re-answering the same card); a recurring need re-earns through DISTINCT tasks (§19 recurrence reading). (5) `OnSession` fires on Drive/DriveResume/DriveStage uniformly — a set seam is never silently ignored; only the stage runner sets it at v0.

## 22. Deliverable/review schema & drain conventions (P3-B4-1; S13.1–S13.4, FC-v1 §2, S02.2/S02.9, S07.6)

- **Package map:** `internal/review` is the S13.1–S13.4 data layer — types/enums/ref namespace/escape-first contract (`review.go`), the host-side `git diff` runner + hunk parser + line map (`diff.go` — ONE diff authority for the surface AND the anchor map), birth validation + the 5-step ladder + finding-anchor parsing (`anchor.go`), the content-addressed object dir (`objects.go`), deliverable/revision store verbs (`store.go`), the comment layer + per-revision placements (`comment.go`), THE drain (`drain.go`), per-type comparison (`compare.go`), PDF extraction (`pdf.go`). Imports storage+eventlog only. `internal/verify` consumes it through the `verify.ReviewSink` seam (`verify/review.go`; adapter `stage/review_sink.go`) — verify never imports review; **nil sink = the pre-S13 in-memory channel** (B2-3/B2-5 shapes preserved, proven by the untouched suites), the composition root always wires it (shell → `stage.Config.Review`; every stage e2e harness wires it identically).
- **Migration 0007** (S13.1 families; exact DDL per the S02.2 workshop mandate): `deliverables` (open `dtype` — NO type refused is a data-layer posture, no CHECK; `subject_ref` for definition deliverables; state moves, identity+lineage don't), `deliverable_revisions` (PK (deliverable,n) 1..N contiguous; immutable via triggers; `snapshot_sha` + `verdict_ref` are the two fill-once NULL slots — S13.5/B4-2 and the verify.round seq; `platform_ref` stores `refs/sinet/deliverable/<id>/rev-<n>`, ref CREATION in the project store is B4-2's), `review_comments` (ONE schema, humans + findings; `comment_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY` so insertion order is explicit, FK-able and dump-stable; original as-claimed anchor immutable; lifecycle CHECKs force the three consumption stamps to appear exactly with `consumed`, one-way), `review_comment_anchors` (status per comment per REVISION; write-once rows — placements are pure functions of immutable revisions; re-validation recomputes and must agree, mismatch = loud error never silent mis-anchor, P-T12-2). All walls are triggers, proven by the raw-SQL battery. **Durable-set membership (S02.9) is structural**: the families live in platform.db (the 11.3 payload IS the S02.2 table set; pipeline B4-3); revision BYTES are host-local in the object dir, outside the payload, loss detectable via the committed hash refs (G2 Def.14).
- **Content pins at B4-1:** both pin kinds retrieve through the content-addressed object dir (`<root>/objects/<aa>/<sha>`, platform-owned — nothing GCs it, S13.1); a single-file content pin hash ≡ the B2-3/B2-5 round-record `SHA256()` (e2e-proven identity), multi-file pins hash the sorted ref manifest (trees arrive with S13.5). Reads always hash-verify (`ErrContentDrift`); minting is idempotent on an identical pin (the S07.7 re-entry), refuses different content under a minted n (`ErrRevisionImmutable`) and non-contiguous n. The stage artifact-store copies stay (B2-5 resume reads them; the same bytes).
- **Minting at the verification handoff (S13.1×S07):** the drain-round top mints the candidate — one per round, `MintRef(run, round)`; a V0-regenerated candidate re-mints as the SAME round's new revision and the malformed mint stays in the never-compressed lineage. Post-round, `RecordVerdict` fills the revision's verdict ref and `RecordFindings` lands the round's findings as open comments — **minus CHECK-INTEGRITY** (suite defects route to card+quarantine, never the review stream, §15) — so S07.6's "notes ride along to the requester as review comments" is durable rows.
- **Anchor model (S13.3/FC-v1 §2):** statuses realized as exact = quote confirmed at the anchored line number itself / mapped = at the diff-mapped position / drifted = found only within ±⚙ `review.anchor_drift_lines` (read per port, never a constant) / file / orphan. Born statuses are exact|drifted|file (mapped is a port outcome; born-orphan doesn't exist — the original-revision link IS the row); a claimed-but-unfound anchor keeps its quote at status file. Server-side re-validation on EVERY render and port (`PlacedComments` recomputes; client/agent positions are never placement authority); old-side anchors port from revision N−1's content, and revision 1's old side (pre-task base) skips the map until S13.5 materializes branch bases — text steps still decide, never a drop. The no-render-location invariant is the CHECK set + the placement totality test; escape-first is recorded as normative data (`EscapeFirst()`, exactly one sanctioned raw-HTML channel, pinned by test; enforcement is S15's).
- **THE drain (S13.4):** `review.Store.Drain` is the ONE path — a repo-wide source-scan conformance test pins that consumption SQL exists in drain.go alone and nothing outside `internal/review` writes `review_comments`. It collects EVERY open comment (any origin revision, ported to current), numbers [F1..Fn] (blockers first, then insertion order — "stable within the attempt" fixes stability, the order is DDL discretion), stamps ONE shared `consumed_at` + consuming attempt ref (`AttemptRef(run, round)` = the revise session identity), emits `review.drained`, and returns the batch — orphaned/file-degraded points INCLUDED (delivery never conditional on anchoring, P-T12-2) and **notes travel** (severity rides every point; only blockers TRIGGER rounds — the verdict law is untouched; nil-sink packages stay blockers-only, the B2-3 shape). The resumed drain (S07.7) records guidance as durable requester comments FIRST, then drains parked still-open findings + guidance in one batch — S13.4's categorical "every open comment" supersedes B2-5's guidance-only package (which deferred revision mechanics to S13); `seed.guidance` still rides the judge's prior-findings scope unchanged.
- **Per-type comparison (S13.2):** behavior host-side in `Compare` — code/text/markdown = `git diff` between revision pins (git adopted as an os-mechanism lock entry at first consumption; absence is a loud error); PDF = extracted-text diff ONLY (adopted `github.com/ledongthuc/pdf`, BSD-3, zero deps, live-verified 2026-07-22 — pdfcpu extracts content streams not reading text, AGPL lanes excluded per G2 Def.13; extraction failure/panics degrade to labeled metadata cards); images = the two-revision object surface (+`PixelDiffAid` seam, nil at v0 — the trio is S15 rendering) + changed-by-hash; binaries = metadata cards + hash verdict; unknown content types = labeled fallback text diff; unknown opaque = labeled cards. NO refusal branch exists.
- **Composer wiring (S13.1 "automation definitions ride the same machinery"):** `dispatchCompose` presents the composed definition AT BIRTH — `dlv-<task>-def`, type `worker-definition`, `subject_ref` = version id, revision 1 = the canonical render via the new hash-verified `worker.Store.VersionSource` (e2e-proven byte-identical to the station-4 card's new side), commentable through the one schema. The B3-2 card keeps its deterministic build; rendering it FROM the review surface is B6's. Definition comments have no rework consumer at v0 (one-shot composer) — they sit open, honestly undrained.
- **Events** `deliverable.minted` / `review.comment` / `review.drained` — refs+hashes only (P-T07-5), run-scoped on the acting run at current generation (§8), platform-scope for run-less human comments (§17 gate precedent); provisional pending the S14 contract (B5), extending the standing §7/§8/§13–§21 note.
- **⚙ consumed:** exactly `review.anchor_drift_lines` (S13.3 step 3), by dotted key through the narrow `review.Settings` interface. The other six S13 ⚙ keys belong to B4-2..B4-4 consumers — declared since B0-2, deliberately untouched.
- **Adoptions (rail):** `PDF text extraction` library entry (ledongthuc/pdf, pinned pseudo-version — an exact pin; funeral plan dslipak/pdf or vendored copy behind the one function; #10 queued for the B4 gate) and `git (host CLI)` os-mechanism entry (GPL-2.0 as an external subprocess — the S13.2 MIT/BSD constraint governs the LINKED extractor; S13.5 is the heavier consumer at B4-2). Fixture: the in-tree `minimalPDF` generator asserts extractor behavior at the pin (S16.4 #5).
- **Clearly-implied readings (section-cited, beyond the above):** (1) severity on HUMAN comments — S13.4's numbered points all carry severity, so the one schema classifies humans too (default note; guidance enters blocking per its "names a problem to fix" nature, B2-5). (2) `Finding.SuggestedChange` added additively — the S13.1 finding schema's optional member, delivered with the numbered point. (3) Drained findings' `Round` = the round the batch FEEDS (stamped by verify at both call sites) — the durable rows are round-agnostic; verify.round events keep per-round numbering. (4) A pre-B4-1 parked card answered under B4-1 refuses loudly at `RecordGuidance` (`ErrNotFound` — no minted lineage exists); acceptable: no production tasks predate the schema (users table empty until operator bootstrap), the B2-5 `ErrNotResumable` precedent. (5) The comparison's old side 0 (pre-task base) renders empty until S13.5 (revision 1 presents against branch base only when git topology can materialize it).

## 23. Git-topology & project-registry conventions (P3-B4-2; S13.5, S13.7, S02.10, S02.4d, S02.6, S05.1/S05.6, S06.2/S06.9, S09.6, S11.3)

- **Package map:** `internal/project` is the S13.5 git topology + S13.7 project/repository registry + the S09.2 knowledge committer. It imports storage + eventlog (+ stdlib) ONLY (AST-pinned, `conformance_test.go`, the §15/§17 precedent): it NEVER imports `internal/{memory,adapters,stage,intake,review,ledger,verify,worker,scheduler}`, and is consumed by stage/intake/review only through narrow func-field/interface seams wired at the composition root (`internal/shell/project_seams.go`). Files: `git.go` (hermetic host-git core), `ignore.go` (structural platform excludes), `registry.go` (repo_registry verbs + match + audit), `scan.go`/`onboard.go` (onboarding-as-task: clone/init → deterministic scan/draft → owner-D10 approval → active; drift check), `workspace.go` (run-branch worktrees, snapshot commits, refs, base content, ff-only advance), `gc.go` (utility checkouts + workspace GC), `committer.go` (`memory.Committer` structurally).
- **Host git is hermetic and per-invocation (S13.5/S13.6 step-2 generalized):** every invocation runs with `GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL=/dev/null GIT_CONFIG_SYSTEM=/dev/null GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM=1 HOME=/nonexistent GIT_TERMINAL_PROMPT=0 GIT_ALLOW_PROTOCOL=file` and `-c gc.auto=0`; identity rides `GIT_AUTHOR_*/GIT_COMMITTER_*` env (snapshots = the platform identity; the committer = the approver, D9). No global/host config is ever read or mutated; only the local file transport is permitted (dev-mode: the remote URL is stored data, never dialed). Behaviour is asserted by fixture tests on THIS host git, never parsed from `--help` (§10 rule 4).
- **Snapshot commits (S13.5):** platform-owned, TREE-LEVEL (`git add -A` captures modified AND untracked bash side effects + deletions), junk-excluded via a platform-owned `core.excludesFile` (structural code data — **no ⚙; S13.5 ratifies none**, the §7 `sseBatchSize` precedent, flagged to the B4 gate), committed on the run branch (fast-forward, never `--amend`). No-change → the prior tip is returned (never `--allow-empty`). "The net is the commits — never" is source-pinned: no `stash`/`--amend`/`--force`/`push`/`jj` string exists in the package (`conformance_test.go`); pushes are S13.6/B4-3's.
- **Branch-per-pipeline (S13.5/S02.10):** one long-lived run branch `sinet/run/<pipeline>` per pipeline, a worktree of the registered project store; each attempt's base (default-branch HEAD **at that attempt's creation**) is recorded durably as `refs/sinet/base/<pipeline>` (attempt 1 — revision 1's old side, S13.1) / `refs/sinet/base/<pipeline>-a<n>` (later attempts) — a fresh attempt branches from the **current, moved** HEAD, never the stale attempt-1 base. `BaseContent` reads attempt 1's base as BYTE-EXACT blobs (`git cat-file blob`, no trim/no synthesized newline — leading blanks, trailing newlines, empty files preserved so Compare line numbers and old-side anchor mapping are exact, P-T12-2). The old attempt's branch stays until retention GC (`CollectRunWorktree(…, requiredRefs)`) clears it — refused when dirty (`ErrDirty`) AND refused unless every minted-revision ref in `requiredRefs` exists + resolves to a reachable commit (`ErrUnprotectedRevision`, never orphaning a minted commit's only copy). Utility checkouts are created LOCKED (a locked worktree survives prune even with its dir absent), never auto-deleted while dirty; workspace GC FLAGS orphan/dirty worktrees, never deletes. Path components are COLLISION-FREE (`pathComponent` hash-suffixes any lossy id). The overlayfs half is S11.3's; this package owns the workspace lifecycle/GC and hands a PATH.
- **Registry & onboarding-as-task (S13.7):** `repo_registry` (migration 0008) holds every S13.7 field; **captured content is VERSIONED** via immutable `repo_registry_captures` rows (re-capture = a new version, never a silent in-place overwrite — trigger-enforced); lifecycle pending→active is one-way (trigger); rows owner-attributed (15.6). **Onboarding is a run task** (`internal/stage/onboard.go`, the `.onboard` role — the `compose.go` precedent): the deterministic register→clone→scan→draft runs over `internal/project` through the `OnboardStart` seam, the draft surfaces on a durable `asks` row ANSWERED BY THE OWNER (D10 — a non-owner is refused, `errOnboardNotOwner`→403), and approval activates the entry (`OnboardApprove`); park/answer ride the existing ask machinery. The scan's force-push danger zone pins the entry's REAL default branch (never a placeholder into ledger §2). The registry feeds are wired: intake resolution (`registrySeam`, S06.2), ledger §2 pinned danger zones, `ProtectedRefs`, preview commands (data), and workspace creation.
- **Run→project resolution (F3/F4):** `runs.workspace_ref` records the workspace (`run.Store.SetWorkspaceRef`, written at execute-leg worktree creation). A run resolves to its project via the DURABLE intake match (`st.Registry.Project`, owner-scoped at S06.2) through `intake.Pipeline.LoadState` — never a fuzzy title re-match, so a cross-user name collision can never supply another user's project. The workspace CWD swap is scoped to EXECUTE legs (`RunSuffixExecute`); intake/verify/compose/helper ceremony sessions stay workspace-less (their checkpoints leave the artifact block empty). `Snapshot` resolves an EXISTING worktree only (the verify mint reads the execute leg's) — never creating one for a ceremony leg. A repo-backed snapshot FAILURE is loud at the sink (never a NULL-indistinguishable content-pin fake, §14).
- **Carried seams closed (B3-report §4):** `memory.Committer` is LIVE (`GitCommitter`: init-if-absent, author=approver via `-c`/env, no-op tolerant, `file_commit` NULL→hash) wired at every `memory.NewGate` site (worker `file_commit` deliberately NOT wired); the S09.6 cited-entry versions join the S02.6 fingerprint via a Plan citation member (additive/`omitempty`, byte-identity preserved) + a shell-wired `CitedEntryVersions` resolver (active-only → mapDrift vanished-key = supersession/removal/new-version drift; a DB error propagates, never mistaken for drift). A citation that cannot resolve at approval is LOUD (`ErrCitationUnresolved`), never silently stored-as-nothing (S09.6 "records those versions"). `intake` never imports `memory` (the resolver is a func seam).
- **⚙ posture:** this packet declares NO new ⚙ key (workspace GC/retention/snapshot cadence/junk rules are STRUCTURAL — S13.5/S02.10 ratify none). The settings-index tally (118 keys) stays green — proof.

## 24. Broker-mediated accept, follow-ups & encrypted snapshots (P3-B4-3; S13.6, S13.9, S13.10, S02.7/S02.8/S02.9, S11.5, S01.7)

- **Package map:** the S13.6 accept ORCHESTRATION is a NEW composition package `internal/accept` (above the storage-only packages, the R27 home): it imports `internal/project` (applies-cleanly/squash/advance/protected-refs), `internal/gates` (the two-phase journal), `internal/broker` (CAS push + SSH sign client), `internal/review` (the accepted state verb) and `internal/run` (the sibling-accept producer) directly, and runs the flow — applies-cleanly depth-1 merge gate → merge card on collision → deterministic trailers → platform squash → class-A effect (Propose/Approve/BeginExecute BEFORE the broker push/Succeed|Fail) → review→accepted + sibling-accept freshness fire. `internal/backup` is the NEW S13.10 pipeline+drill package (imports storage + eventlog + broker client + the age/zstd modules + stdlib; `settings` only in the one-shot `mode.go` entry; NEVER `internal/{memory,adapters,stage,intake}`, AST-pinned). The accept git verbs live in `internal/project` (`accept.go`: `AppliesClean`/`SquashAccept`/`AdvanceDefaultBranch`), the state verb in `internal/review` (`accept.go`: `Accept`/`AcceptedRevision`), the follow-up link in `internal/intake` (`followup.go`), the sibling-accept producer in `internal/run` (`FireSiblingAccept`).
- **The broker is the guardrail (P-T12-1):** `internal/broker` gains git verbs (`OpPush`, `OpSignData`, kind `git-ssh-key`) staying stdlib + `x/sys` (git/ssh-keygen via `os/exec`, no DB). Every push is CAS (`--force-with-lease=<ref>:<expect>`; a bare/value-less lease is REFUSED before any git runs); a push to a protected ref (S13.7 registry policy passed as request DATA) requires `Authorized=true`, which only the control-plane accept/snapshot path sets — the mandatory refusal test (`TestP_T12_1_ProtectedRefRefusal`) is a standing `internal/broker` entry. A stale-lease REJECTION is `Rejected=true` (a normal collision → merge card, NEVER a blind retry); a crash-window in-doubt row replays blind against the SAME pinned expect-sha (class-A `ReconcileInDoubt`). The git-ssh-key NEVER leaves the broker — decrypted only into a 0600 temp file in the broker's own 0700 dir, used, removed; only the result (the push outcome / the armored SSHSIG) returns. `gitsign` NEVER appears in the package (source-scan `TestNoGitsignString`).
- **SSH signing (S13.6 step 5):** the broker SSHSIG-signs commit bytes (`ssh-keygen -Y sign -n git`); `internal/project` builds the commit object and embeds the signature as the `gpgsig` header (the payload signed is the object WITHOUT the header, exactly what git re-verifies) — the signature is verify-clean (`TestSquashAcceptSigned`/`TestAcceptSigned`). Signing is a STRUCTURAL per-user posture (drain F3), NOT a per-call flag: `accept.SigningPosture(user)` derives from the user's broker git-ssh-key presence (secret-free `OpHasKey`); the accept path has no signing argument, so the platform can never produce a mixed-signed history (`TestSigningPostureIsStructural`). The operator signs from day one at bring-up.
- **Drain round 1 refinements (F1–F9):** the accept + follow-up surfaces are CONSTRUCTED at the shell composition root (`buildAcceptSurface`, held by the api layer for the B6 endpoints — compiled in and reachable, `go list -deps ./cmd/sinet` includes `internal/accept`; driven by `TestBuildAcceptSurfaceComposition` through the same wiring, no test-local mirror). The depth-1 merge queue validates a second in-flight accept onto HEAD-plus-first (an in-flight registry keyed per project; `AdvanceDefaultBranch` re-reads the local head and tolerates an already-incorporated commit for the stacked case). The class-A accept effect's payload is self-sufficient (expect-sha + candidate + trailers + subject/provenance/author) so `Accepter.ReDrive`/`ReDriveApproved` re-drive an in-doubt accept after `ReconcileInDoubt` blind against the SAME pinned expect-sha (wired at shell startup after `ladder.Reconcile`); the squash timestamp is pinned to the effect's approval time for a deterministic replay. `onCalendarFromSeconds` is total over the ⚙ clamp (never a generation failure); `DumpFrom` fails LOUD on an unrecognized FTS-prefixed table (never a silent drop).
- **Attribution trailers (S13.6 step 3, §8 reading 2):** the trailer TEMPLATE is a STRUCTURAL string constant in `internal/accept` (`Co-Authored-By: <engine> <model> <vendor-noreply>` + `Assisted-by: <engine> (<model>) via Sinet`) — S18 ratifies no trailer key and its tally is frozen at 118, so this is the `sseBatchSize`/auth-constant precedent (settings-tab directive, flagged to the B4 gate), NOT an invented ⚙ key. The RENDERED trailers are pinned in the class-A effect payload at approval (deterministic, byte-stable, audit-trailed, displayed pre-accept).
- **Snapshot pipeline (S13.10):** the S13.10 snapshot/restore triad lives at the STORAGE seam (`internal/storage/backup.go`: `VacuumInto`/`DumpFrom`/`RebuildFromDump`/`CheckRestored`) because the modernc driver's only importer is `internal/storage` (§6) — `internal/backup` orchestrates through them and never touches the driver. Durable set → `VACUUM INTO` (consistent copy) → text `.dump` (FTS5 externals rebuilt from content, run_events trace bodies past the 11.1 horizon elided by seq — v0 elides nothing) + git-versioned file-store text exports → `tar | zstd | age -r <recipients>` → ONE encrypted blob → committed to the operator-owned snapshot repo (keep-N by ledger seq, robust to same-timestamp bases) → broker CAS push. The `snapshot_ledger` integrity triple (SHA-256 + size + created-at) records in platform.db AND — via durable-set membership — inside every SUBSEQUENT archive (P-T12-4). Blob ≤ ~90 MB chunks (single-chunk household norm; the multi-chunk path is tested).
- **Encryption is unconditional; secrets excluded (S13.10):** the pipeline reads platform.db + the file stores ONLY, never the broker credential store (`Config` has no broker-store field) — a snapshot with zero recipients is REFUSED. Identity + recipient come from a config path (default `~/.config/sinet/age-identity.txt`), the recipient DERIVED at runtime (`LoadCrypto`); tests use fresh temp keypairs and NEVER read the real file; NO key material is hardcoded anywhere.
- **Restore drill (S13.10):** fetch the newest blob from the REMOTE → verify against the ledger FAIL-CLOSED (a mismatch aborts before decrypt, records a failure, raises a High flag — the S14 seam) → decrypt with the ESCROW identity → unpack → rebuild into a SCRATCH DB (the live DB is never rebuilt) → assert integrity + the S02.9 invariants (fk-consistency, gap-free `event_seq`) → an event-log record EITHER way.
- **Cadence = persistent calendar timers (S01.7):** one-shot `sinet snapshot` / `sinet restore-drill` modes (`internal/backup/mode.go`, dispatched in `internal/cli`), driven by GENERATED-never-installed `sinet-snapshot.timer`/`.service` + `sinet-restore-drill.timer`/`.service` (`Persistent=true`; `OnCalendar` derived from ⚙ `backup.interval`/`backup.drill_interval`) — NOT in-process tickers. The LIVE first push is a bring-up step (S19.6), PURE CONFIG (remote URL via `EnvironmentFile`, broker git-ssh-key profile, escrow identity path) — no code change to go live; the executor performs NO network push. `backup.repo_rotation` referenced, not consumed (rotation = an operator ceremony).
- **Follow-ups (S13.9):** migration 0009 adds `snapshot_ledger` (append-only, integrity-triple) + `task_successor_of` (a link table with a composite FK to `deliverable_revisions(deliverable_id, n)` so the source revision is referentially real; bidirectional lineage via the PK and the indexed source pair). `intake.FollowUp.Spawn` creates the successor task + link in ONE action and (via a `Start` seam) enters it into normal intake with inherited context + the predecessor ref as brief material; revision/extension/counterpart are intake PRESETS over the SAME link (`presetFraming`), never schema variants. Concurrent follow-ups ride the EXISTING S02.8 claims machinery (B2-2) — NO new claims code; an accepted sibling fires the freshness trigger through the accept-success producer (`run.FireSiblingAccept` feeding the shipped `EvaluateFreshness` `SiblingAccept` consumer).
- **Adoptions (rail):** EXACTLY two — `filippo.io/age` v1.3.1 (rides the EXISTING S16.3 age row; BSD-3; `modules` cover age + the live-resolved transitive `filippo.io/hpke` — NOT edwards25519, the live-verify caught the memory guess) and `github.com/klauspost/compress` v1.19.1 (NET-NEW S16.4 onboarding, the one adoption beyond a pre-registered row; pure-Go/cgo-free confirmed under `CGO_ENABLED=0`; BSD-3 path-scoped over the zstd subtree). Both pins LIVE-verified 2026-07-22 against proxy.golang.org; full S16.4 walks in the lock entries; operator #10 ratification QUEUED to the B4 gate. NO new engine adoption; the `git (host CLI)` os-mechanism entry is untouched. The 118-key S18 tally stays green (NO new ⚙ key declared).
- **⚙ consumed** (by dotted key, never a constant): `backup.interval` (snapshot timer), `backup.keep` (keep-N), `backup.drill_interval` (drill timer). Referenced-not-consumed: `backup.repo_rotation` (operator ceremony), `effects.approval_expiry` (gates-owned, applies to the accept effect at `BeginExecute`).

## 25. Preview module & port-pool conventions (P3-B4-4; S13.8, S13.5/S13.7, S01.2/S01.4/S01.5, S11.1/S11.3/S11.8, S16.2–S16.4)

- **Package map:** `internal/portpool` is the S13.8 preview port allocator (file-backed O_EXCL reservations over a SMALL fixed range — `portpool.go`) plus the S01.2 `sinet-portpool.service` daemon (loopback/UDS JSON serve loop + client + loopback self-assert — `serve.go`; the `sinet portpool` mode — `mode.go`). It imports **stdlib only**. `internal/preview` is the S13.8 module: type dispositions + runner heuristics (`disposition.go`), netns port probing (`probe.go`), the Caddy admin client + port-based route generation (`caddy.go`), the socket-proxyd unit TEXT (`units.go`), the lifecycle manager (`manager.go`), the ttyd invocation (`ttyd.go`), and the before-vs-after synced-navigation contract (`comparison.go` + types in `preview.go`). The `portpool` mode left the `internal/cli` reserved table (now empty — every S01.2 daemon mode is built); the shell wires the preview surface at the composition root (`internal/shell/preview_seams.go`, the `buildAcceptSurface` precedent — compiled in, `go list -deps ./cmd/sinet` includes `internal/preview`).
- **Fixed port range + pre-installed static units is the grant-compatible production shape (§8 reading 1):** the S11.8 grant explicitly does NOT include `manage-unit-files`, so `sinet` can never install per-preview dynamic units. A SMALL fixed range (`portpool.DefaultRangeLo/Hi`, structural) makes the `.socket`+proxyd pairs static files installed ONCE at the gate; the packet EMITS unit text parameterized by pool port (`RenderProxydUnits` → `--exit-idle-time`=⚙ `preview.idle_stop`, loopback `ListenStream`) and GENERATES-never-installs. Idle-stop in this no-backend-unit topology is `--exit-idle-time` + the `.socket` re-arm (the proxy exits when idle, the socket re-listens, the next connection re-activates it); `StopWhenUnneeded` is deliberately NOT set — nothing `Requires=` the proxy, so it would let systemd stop a just-activated proxy (drain F9). The ⚙ idle value flows through the composition-wired `Manager.RenderUnits` (the production unit-generation path for the gate-install flow; F1), not just the test. `systemd-socket-proxyd` is host systemd (host-managed) — NO lock entry.
- **Previews are READ-ONLY consumers of settled substrate (S13.8; R24 import wall, AST-pinned):** `internal/preview` imports `internal/{project,review,sandbox,units,eventlog}` (+ `portpool`/`storage`/`settings` at most) and NEVER `internal/{adapters,stage,intake,memory,broker,gates,verify,worker,scheduler}` — no minting/draining/accepting, no engine/effect machinery. Composition rides the EXISTING `sandbox.BuildPlan`/`Compose` surface as a C2-shaped preview (rw workspace + registries-allowlist-as-data; empty netns until the deferred egress substrate lands) — no new class, no loosened profile, no extra binds beyond the checkout+clone, no egress widening. An uncomposable host is the honest `unavailable` state, never an unconfined run.
- **Zero mutation (S13.8; R6):** the preview substrate is a locked worktree-at-rev **utility checkout** (`project.Store.AddUtilityCheckout` at the revision's `SnapshotSHA`/`PlatformRef`), read-only; ALL writable lanes (dev-server AND CLI/ttyd, drain F2) compose a **throwaway clone** (`copyTree`, `.git` worktree-pointer skipped) — never the checkout — deleted at teardown; clones live under a platform-owned scratch root (`<state-dir>/preview-clones`; `t.TempDir()` in tests), never system temp (F16). The reviewed run worktree is NEVER handed to a preview. Teardown `ReleaseUtilityCheckout` refuses a dirtied checkout (a loud defect). Zero-mutation is proven through the REAL Manager launch→compose→teardown path (symlinks hashed, F3). The bwrap `--overlay`/`--ro-overlay`/`--tmp-overlay` upper is the production alternative substrate: its flag acceptance on this host's bwrap 0.11.1 is **behavior-asserted** (`TestBwrapOverlayFlags`, §10), but the workspace-overlay WIRING (S11.3/S02.10) is a deferred host component — the clone is the v0 path (F15).
- **Netns port probing (S13.8; R8):** the SHIPPED `probeListeningPorts` parses the sandboxed process **TREE's** `/proc/<pid>/net/tcp{,6}` LISTEN rows (walks descendants — bwrap's monitor is in the HOST netns while the listener is a descendant sharing the new empty netns; probing the monitor alone would read the host table incl. 8481; the tree walk is production code, not test-only, drain F5), never trusting config. Pure parser fixture-tested; a caps-gated live probe consumes the production function and HARD-FAILS (never skips) when the listener does not appear despite caps + a started bwrap (F4), proving the netns semantics against a real bwrap empty netns (forcing the native path — srt removes the netns). Multi-port → picker DATA; single port auto-selects.
- **Caddy admin-API usage contained in the module (S13.8; R12/R13):** route add-on-launch / remove-on-teardown, idempotent both ways, against a CONFIGURED admin endpoint (dev default UNSET → routing disabled/no-op, recorded on the state); routes are subdomain/port-based, NEVER path-prefix, and preserve the WebSocket upgrade/Connection header flow; the runner invocation carries the per-provider allowedHosts injection (Vite `server.allowedHosts` on the pnpm/npm lane, or Vite HMR dies). Tests dial a local httptest fake ONLY — the LIVE host Caddy admin API (fronting 8481/8482) is never dialed (host hazard; an AST string-literal tripwire enforces it).
- **Runner tools are NOT adoptions (S13.8/S16.2; R2):** pnpm/npm/uv/mise are detected per-project (four spec-named detections; registry preview command wins over heuristics, R3) and executed inside the sandbox — an absent tool is the honest `no-preview` state ("required tool not present"), NEVER an install. **ttyd IS the packet's one adoption** — a NET-NEW `os-mechanism` lock entry (full S16.4 ten-check walk; pin **1.7.7 LIVE-verified 2026-07-22**; MIT at the ref), probe-gated (PATH + `SINET_TTYD_PATH`), stub-tested; **operator #10 + the host-install decision are QUEUED to the B4 gate** — if declined, the no-preview state IS the behavior with zero code change. Anything beyond ttyd would stop-and-flag. **Static server = stdlib `http.FileServer`, no adoption.**
- **Low-tier, disposable, no durable state (S13.8; R22/R23):** launching a preview is Low-tier reversible — NO effect journal, NO approval, NO PIN step-up; lifecycle events (`preview.started`/`preview.stopped`, provisional pending S14) are platform-scope (run_id NULL, no generation — previews are not runs), owner-attributed (15.6), refs+ids not blobs. NO migration (previews have no durable table — live state is the in-memory manager map + the event trace); migrations stay 0001–0009.
- **⚙ consumed** (by dotted key through the narrow `preview.Settings`, never constants): `preview.idle_stop` (proxyd `--exit-idle-time`), `preview.max_concurrent` (the manager's at-capacity refusal — port-holding sessions only; arbitration is S10's, refusal suffices). **No new ⚙ key** (the 118-key S18 tally stays green — proof): the Caddy admin endpoint, the preview base host, and the port range are **structural deployment config** (composition-root override via env/config at bring-up; the `SINET_SRT_PATH` precedent), flagged to the B4 gate under the settings-tab directive.
- **Dev-mode vs LIVE (host hazard):** the executor's whole surface is hermetic — fake Caddy admin (httptest), in-process/file-stub portpool, stub `ttyd`, fixture trees + fixture proc files + a caps-gated live netns probe, local utility checkouts on `t.TempDir()`. LIVE wiring (hardening session + egress/socket-activation substrate; pre-installed static `.socket`+proxyd pairs + `sinet-portpool.service`; Caddy admin-endpoint config; `tailscale serve` for the preview range; ttyd host-install IF #10 ratifies) is a bring-up/gate sequence — the executor performs none of it. The STATIC lane + routing + portpool + unit generation go live by pure config; the **dev-server/CLI lanes additionally need lane-activation CODE at bring-up** (the spawn/probe/route path over the socket-activation substrate) — their v0 state is the honest DEFERRED reason, NOT a zero-code flip (drain F6). Structural config (Caddy admin endpoint, base host, port range via `SINET_PREVIEW_*`) is composition-root passthrough, no ⚙ key.

## 26. Local-models tier conventions (P3-B4-5; S12.1–S12.4, S12.8, S03.2, S06.10)

- **Package map:** `internal/local` is the S12 platform-plane surface of the permanent free tier — the duty-alias `/v1` client + request shaping (`client.go`: temp-0 greedy, `response_format:json_schema`, logprobs), alias resolution over ⚙ + the manifest (`alias.go`), the committed model manifest (`manifest.go`), llama-swap config generation + the one-shot nvidia-smi UUID read (`config.go`), the class-(b) duty caller + D7 metering (`duty.go`), the usage marker (`usage.go`), the eager-unload verb + admission gate + surface (`eagerunload.go`), the GameMode legs (`gamemode.go`), the duty schemas (`schema.go`), the structural-config reader + installed-stack detection (`conformance.go`), and the tier-F fake (`fake.go`). Imports **storage/eventlog/gates/settings + stdlib ONLY** (AST-pinned, `importwall_test.go`): NEVER `adapters/stage/intake/worker/verify/memory/broker/scheduler/review/project/metering`. The class-(b) seam ADAPTERS (`intake.Classifier`/`Utility`/`SpotCheck`, `worker.TieBreaker`) live in `internal/stage/local.go` (which already imports intake/worker) — the `internal/stage/router.go` bridge precedent (§19); `worker` never imports `local` (also AST-pinned).
- **Consumer-class split BINDS (S12.1; §8 reading 3):** this cut ships only **class (b)** — the control plane invokes S12.4 duty aliases directly, no engine session (intake triage, tie-break, utility drafting). **Class (a)** — a run executing ON a local model via the opencode adapter — has **NO v0 consumer** (no second adapter this cut). So a template/helper dispatch onto duty `utility`/mechanical resolves to the paid execution seat with the refined honest reason ("its engine lane carries no v0 consumer — platform duty calls ride it directly"), never an error, never a fake dispatch onto a lane with no adapter (`routing.go` `resolveSeat` — the B3-3 ERROR stub RETIRED). The effective DutyMap gains the `DutyUtility` seat (lane `local`, manifest window) and `Coverage.LocalAvailable` flips true when the stack is configured; the class-(a) path degrades regardless.
- **$0 metering (R18/R19; §8 readings 4+5):** every local call writes ONE D7 checkpoint row (`gates.Checkpoints`) on the **consuming run** (intake seams + tie-break ride `<task>.intake`), whose usage block carries a `local` MARKER — a JSON WIRE CONTRACT read by `internal/metering` (neither package imports the other): `{"input_tokens":N,"output_tokens":M,"local":{"lane":"local","duty":…,"model":…,"model_sha256":…,"engine_build":…}}`. The metering fold AND the S10.4 pressure gauge HONOR the per-row lane override (lane is otherwise joined from `runs`), so a local call meters on the free tier, not the consuming run's paid lane. Lane `local` prices a **TRUE $0** (labeled zero-allowance, `LineItem.ZeroAllowance()`), NOT UNPRICED — explicitly distinct from the S10.3 never-price-a-flat-lane-from-$0 guard, which continues to bind PAID flat-rate lanes (pinned side by side, `metering/local_test.go`). A call site with no run in a checkpointable state is a SURFACED DEFECT (loud log + `local.unmetered_defect` platform event), never a silently unmetered call.
- **Duty shaping (S12.4):** temp-0 greedy for classification-shaped duties; the schema in the prompt AND enforced at the engine (`json_schema`; GBNF where a schema cannot express it — behaviorally probed at the pin, never docs); every CLASSIFICATION schema carries an explicit abstain/`unclear` member by construction (`schema.go`) + a belt (`assertAbstain`); free-text drafting (utility Help) is `Classification:false` (no forced-label abstain); per-duty length caps are structural constants (`triageMaxTokens` etc., §8 reading 7). Logprobs are requested/exposed on this plane (the only v0 logprob lane — S03.2/SPIKE P2-S1; consumers B4-7 margins + B5 drift canary). Degradation (R17): a caller error surfaces to the seam and each degrades per its S12.4/S06 row — classifier fails closed (high), utility falls back to deterministic text, spot-check skipped, tie-break the deterministic order — never faked.
- **Alias registry (R15):** ⚙ `local.alias` (TypeMap) resolved by dotted key AT CALL TIME (map-family discipline, §6); the map is read-only data (swaps land only through the S12.10 gate, B4-7 — no swap machinery here); the `embedder` alias refuses with the post-gate reason (G2 Def.8 not opened); unknown alias = loud error.
- **Model set = platform data, never `components.lock` (§8 reading 6; S16.1):** the committed `Manifest()` carries per seat {seat, model, card URL, license {SPDX-or-card-name, verify date}, file(s)+sha256, quant, pool, context length, servable}. Licenses verified ON THE CARD at pull time; Apache-2.0/MIT default; Bespoke-MiniCheck-7B is the sole ratified CC-BY-NC-4.0 exception (per-seat, with reason); a card-vs-spec conflict is RECORDED as the card states + FLAGGED (`License.Conflict`), never silently adopted (the Gemma 4 row). Model WEIGHTS are never committed — they live in the shared read-only model-cache root (structural config, operator-relocatable, gate-flagged). The manifest hash is what R18 rows and B4-7 keying consume. Non-servable CPU seats (DeBERTa NLI, Flan-T5) + the post-gate embedder are recorded honestly `Servable:false` and NEVER faked into the generated config (R10).
- **Generated llama-swap unit (§8 reading 1; R11):** `sinet-llamaswap.service` IS generated by `internal/units` (the B0-era "local-model units … not generated here" doc line CORRECTED, cited to S12.2 + the STATE directive) — llama-swap ships no unit file, so the unit is SINET config for an unmodified adopted binary (S16.1, not a fork); `ExecStart` runs the operator-installed llama-swap binary + generated config (structural config, NOT the sinet binary — no new `cmd/`/mode); loopback bind; `User=sinet`; the S01.2 hardening set with the recorded GPU exception (PrivateDevices NOT set — backends need `/dev/nvidia*`); `Slice=sinet-local.slice` (the systemd-oomd victim; a minimal slice with NO invented oomd/weight policy — that POLICY is S10's, deferred). GENERATED never installed (install = B4 gate/hardening). Caddy/changedetection/tailscaled stay un-generated (they ship their own units).
- **Eager-unload + GameMode (R12/R13/R14):** the eager-unload verb stops local-lane admissions + calls `POST /api/models/unload`, idempotent + event-recorded, surfaced as card DATA (B6) + the `sinet local` CLI tool (a tool, never a mode; unload leg direct to llama-swap, admission-stop rides the control-plane surface — dev posture documented). The GameMode hook is ⚙ `local.gamemode_hook`-gated: the generated `gamemode.ini` `[custom]` snippet (rendered output + a documented operator install step, NEVER written to a home dir) + a `busctl`-subprocess D-Bus subscription (zero new Go deps — §8 reading 8). The R14 probe (`P3/measurements/2026-07-22-gamemode-user-context-probe.md`) BOUND leg (b): GameMode signals are per-user SESSION-bus, unreachable from the `sinet` system user without the operator's `DBUS_SESSION_BUS_ADDRESS` — so the subscription runs only when that address is supplied (bring-up config), else deferred-with-finding; the scripts leg carries pause/resume regardless.
- **⚙ consumed** (dotted key, never constants): `local.alias` (TypeMap; R15/R20–R22), `local.ttl.fast_s` + `local.ttl.workhorse_s` (config-gen TTLs; R10), `local.gamemode_hook` (R13), and `local.unload.term_grace_s` consumed as the per-model llama-swap `unloadTimeout` (the R10-comment optional consumption — the live schema exposes the knob). **Referenced, not consumed** (named seams): `local.ttl.egpu_s` (pool24 dormant), `local.vram.guard_band_mb` + `local.battery.gpu_admission` + `local.broker.sandbox_logprobs` (B4-6), `local.batch.ac_only` + `local.reeval.cadence_months` (B4-7). **No new ⚙ key** (the 118-key tally stays green — proof of R28); llama-swap endpoint/binary/config/model-cache paths + `SINET_LOCAL_*` overrides + the GameMode bus address + per-duty length caps + the slice name are structural config/constants (composition-root passthrough — the `SINET_SRT_PATH` precedent, gate-flagged under the settings-tab directive).
- **Conformance tiers (R25–R27):** tier F (the `local.FakeServer`, always), tier R (real llama-swap+llama.cpp when installed, $0, auto-runs; absence prints `SANCTIONED SKIP (CONVENTIONS §10)` — MECHANIZED: starts the pinned llama-swap on an ephemeral port against a generated config and asserts the /v1/models + unload contract, plus a LOUD installed-vs-pin delta notice, §10/drain F3), tier L (the ONE live smoke under `SINET_LIVE_SMOKE=1`, $0 by construction — asserts json_schema ENFORCEMENT against a hostile prompt, logprobs PRESENT, the $0 D7 row's model hash + engine build, and VRAM release after eager-unload). `LlamaSwapPin`/`LlamaCppPin` are exported + test-coupled to the lock entries (`TestPinsMatchLock`); an installed-vs-pin delta is reported LOUDLY, never retargeted (P-T15-2). NO migration (0001–0009 stand); NO new engine adoption beyond the three rail entries; zero new Go modules.
- **Drain round 1 (P3-B4-5 F1–F12) — EXECUTED, not deferred:** the sanctioned legs ran user-level (never a driver/system change): cmake (Kitware tarball) + the CUDA 12.9 toolkit (assembled from the runfile `builds/`, toolkit-only, user path) + gcc-14 (micromamba, since host gcc-15 exceeds CUDA 12.9's ≤14 support) → llama.cpp b10085 built with CUDA for **sm_120** (the Blackwell RTX 5070 Ti); the model set pulled to the cache with real per-file sha256 for the seats that matter (workhorse-default Qwen3.5-9B, fast/smoke Qwen3.5-4B, CPU-floor Flan-T5), the rest recorded as HONEST upstream blockers, not re-deferred (Arctic/Gemma/Granite hit HF's per-IP rate-limit after ~15 GB; Bespoke 401-gated under CC-BY-NC; DeBERTa no-GGUF); the REAL tier-L smoke passed on the GPU ($0). Fixes: tie-break D7 rides `q.RunID` (the consuming run, not a hard-coded `<task>.intake` — helper-spawn context, F2); durable cross-process admission flag (`SINET_LOCAL_STATE`, restart-surviving — the CLI + surface share it, F4/F11); ordered `reason`-first schemas (free-text-then-constrained — b10085 preserves property order, confirmed live, F5); classification duties disable a reasoning model's `<think>` phase (`chat_template_kwargs enable_thinking:false`) so the length cap yields the constrained JSON; the spot-check rides the `intake-triage` alias per S12.4 (spec over the brief's R20, F8); per-seat bounded `ServingContext` in config-gen (the model's full context OOMs the 12 GB pool, F9); the empty `resident` group is materialized (v241 accepts it, F10); `nvidia-persistenced`-never + `-lgc`-operator-flag documented (F6); the operator's own ollama.service runs outside platform management (F6).

## 27. GPU broker (two planes) + VRAM ledger/admission/preemption (P3-B4-6; S12.6–S12.8, S10.9, S11.5–S11.6)

- **The GPU broker is a DISTINCT component from the credential broker (S12.6 term note):** this section always writes "GPU broker" — the control-plane-mediated local-inference DATA plane. It EXTENDS `internal/local` (new files `gpubroker.go` two-plane surface + token registry + sandboxed-plane `/v1` handler; `vram.go` sleep-gated ledger + admitter; `preempt.go` kill-not-freeze). It is NOT `internal/broker` (the S00-glossary credential broker, SO_PEERCRED). The distinctness is a CODE FACT: `internal/local` never imports `internal/broker` (the §26 import wall + `importwall_test.go` pin it). No proxy product is adopted (virtual-key PATTERN only) → zero new `components.lock` entries (stays 17).
- **Two caller planes (S12.6):** **platform plane** = the existing B4-5 loopback `client.go` — ALL management verbs (load/unload/config) live ONLY here. **Sandboxed plane** = `SandboxBroker.Handler()` — EXACTLY `POST /v1/chat/completions` + `POST /v1/embeddings` + `GET /v1/models`; every `/api/*` management verb is 404 by construction (a `http.ServeMux` with only the three routes). `/v1/models` returns ONLY the run's allowlist; the `model` field carries a duty ALIAS (resolved via `Registry.SeatFor`, exactly as the platform plane), never a raw id; `/v1/embeddings` refuses with the post-gate reason (route exists, embedder seat does not serve). Identity is a per-run bearer token minted at spawn (`TokenRegistry.Mint`→`Resolve` = token→run→owner; in-memory, run-lifetime — NO migration). Logprobs are refused unless BOTH the run's grant AND live ⚙ `local.broker.sandbox_logprobs` are on (side-channel reduction). Every sandboxed call writes ONE $0 D7 row keyed to the SANDBOXED run, REUSING the B4-5 `usage.go` wire contract — a new WRITER, NO metering-package change (R17).
- **The sandboxed plane is a SEAM, not a live path (BINDING):** S12.1 class (a) has no v0 consumer (no second engine adapter this cut) + the AppArmor userns finding blocks service-context confined runs, so NOTHING mints a token from a real spawn. The mint/resolve/handler and the bridge injection are HERMETICALLY tested (a fake spawn mints; a fake sandboxed call presents) — faking a live consumer would violate the BINDING reading + §19. The shell COMPOSES the broker (held dormant); no per-sandbox listener is served this cut.
- **Bridge injection rides the spawn seam (OQ3, the `Confiner`/`CredInject` precedent):** `adapters.StartRequest.BridgeGrant *BridgeGrant` is `json:"-"` — recompiled every run, NEVER in a park record/log/DB. The confinement class decides the grant: **C0 connectors NEVER get the bridge** (`TokenRegistry.Mint` refuses C0 AND `sandbox.Compose` fails closed on a C0 bridge), **C1/C2 standard**, **C3 tighter budgets** — the class→budget policy `sandbox.BridgeBudgetFor` lives in `internal/sandbox` (the ladder's home; a class comparison, never re-derived across the wall). The sandbox side (`Compose`) binds the per-sandbox UDS read-write + `--setenv`s the worker's `/v1` at it — never `/dev/nvidia*`, never a routable host address. `internal/local` mints the token/allowlist but never imports `adapters`/`sandbox`; `internal/sandbox → adapters` stays the only edge.
- **VRAM ledger = per-machine MEASUREMENTS, machinery-here / calibration-B4-7 (S12.7; OQ1 machinery-only):** the R09-OQ8 protocol is built (UUID/PCI enumeration from sysfs; **sleep-gate EVERY read** — `power/runtime_status` FIRST, a suspended GPU recorded "asleep, 0 client bytes" and NEVER polled; device-truth `memory.free`; per-process attribution INCLUDING graphics clients via `nvidia-smi -q -x` process_info — `--query-compute-apps` misses them, live-verified). The per-model FOOTPRINT VALUES + compositor headroom (hybrid/MUX) + kill→free latencies are `TBD-BRINGUP` → B4-7 (S12.9 #2). The ledger ships **honest-uncalibrated**: `Footprint` returns `calibrated=false` for every model, so admission rests ENTIRELY on the live reading. Re-run triggers (driver/engine/model-quant/display-mode change) are documented data (`LedgerReRunTriggers`), NOT a periodic job.
- **Admission math (S12.7/R9):** admitted iff **live `memory.free` ≥ ledger(model) + ⚙ `local.vram.guard_band_mb`** — always the LIVE reading, never the belief. A suspended GPU (0 client bytes) admits (loading wakes it, S12.8). **After any forced kill, `memory.free` recovery MUST be verified before the next admission** (zombie VRAM): a kill sets a recovery-pending gate that refuses admission until the live free returns to the post-kill target.
- **NO periodic GPU polling anywhere (S12.8/R15):** reads are EVENT-DRIVEN — they run only when a load/admission is contemplated or a kill's recovery is verified, never on a timer; no goroutine wakes the GPU. The sysfs `runtime_status`/power-supply reads and `nvidia-smi` runs are injectable seams (`gpuReader`/`powerReader`/`smiFunc`) so a suspended-GPU fixture proves the sleep gate never polls. `nvidia-persistenced` is NEVER run; `-lgc` clock caps are a flagged operator/hardening item, never applied.
- **Kill-not-freeze is the GPU tool (S12.7/R11):** freezing keeps the CUDA context — only teardown frees VRAM. `preempt.go` applies **SIGTERM → ⚙ `local.unload.term_grace_s` → SIGKILL** (the kill path is MANDATORY, proven against a SIGTERM-ignoring backend), then verifies recovery. It sits BEHIND the B4-5 eager-unload verb (`EagerUnload.SetPreemptor` → `ForceKillWedged` finds a wedged `llama-server` still holding VRAM after llama-swap's unload and force-kills it). Freeze/thaw stays the CPU/RAM tool (S10), never used for VRAM.
- **Both admission call sites (OQ2 BOTH):** the LIVE platform-plane duty pre-flight (`Duty.Call` runs `VRAMAdmitter.AdmitDuty` before any llama-swap load a duty triggers; nil-safe no-gate when unconfigured; a refusal surfaces `ErrGPUAdmissionRefused` and degrades per the duty's S12.4/§26 row, never a faked load) AND the scheduler's nil-able **GPU-admission POLICY hook** (`scheduler.GPUAdmitter`, the `Pressure`/`Dispatcher` precedent; satisfied structurally by `*local.VRAMAdmitter` wired at the shell — `internal/scheduler` NEVER imports `internal/local`; gated on `LocalLane` which has no v0 consumer → wired-but-dormant). Battery admission (⚙ `local.battery.gpu_admission` {never, urgent-only, always}) is consulted in the same pre-flight; battery/AC is a sleep-safe host observation (`type=Mains` power-supply `online`), never a periodic wake; per-duty urgency is a structural constant (watchdog-verdict = urgent; batch-class = not — `aliasUrgent`).
- **⚙ consumed** (dotted key, never constants): `local.vram.guard_band_mb` (R9), `local.unload.term_grace_s` (the Sinet-side kill grace, R11 — B4-5 already consumed it as llama-swap's `unloadTimeout`), `local.battery.gpu_admission` (R14), `local.broker.sandbox_logprobs` (R5). **No new ⚙ key** (118 tally green). Rate/size budgets, admission/recovery timings, the bridge socket path, and the local-lane name passed to the scheduler are structural constants/config (the `sseBatchSize` precedent), NOT invented ⚙.
- **Conformance tiers (§10):** tier F fixtures always (injectable VRAM readings incl. a suspended fixture, the sandboxed-plane handler, the SIGKILL/recovery path against a real SIGTERM-ignoring child); tier L the live core under `SINET_LIVE_SMOKE=1` ($0) — the sleep-gated `runtime_status` read, device-truth `memory.free`, per-process attribution, admission on live readings, and the SIGTERM→grace→SIGKILL of a REAL `llama-server` backend with `memory.free` recovery verified. The sandboxed plane STAYS hermetic (no live confined consumer — BINDING + AppArmor). NO migration (0001–0009 stand); zero new lock entries (17); zero new Go modules.

## 28. Confidence calibration + T15 battery + swap gate conventions (P3-B4-7; S12.5, S12.9, S12.10, S09.7, S07.4)

- **The S12.5 confidence signal is the label-token logprob margin, never verbalized confidence (R1):** `margin.go` computes `LabelMargin(logprobs, fields)` = the MINIMUM, across a duty's routing-decisive label fields, of the top1−top2 gap at each field's first value token (reconstructs the emitted text token-by-token, locates the value token — for an enum the first token commits the choice; the least-confident label is the case margin). It reads the top-2 logprobs the B4-5 client already requests (`client.go TopLogprobs=2`). **No code path anywhere reads a self-reported confidence field** — the signal is structural. A missing label token returns ok=false (the margin is honestly unavailable, never fabricated). The SAME scalar feeds calibration (R25) and the live re-check (R4), so they never disagree.
- **The four-step S12.5 recipe, stdlib-only, every number re-derived (R2/R3):** `calibrate.go` — (1) a labeled set (`LabeledItem{Margin, Wrong}`), (2) margins via `LabelMargin`, (3) `IsotonicFit` = pool-adjacent-violators (monotone NON-increasing P(error) in margin; no Go module), (4) `Fit(key, items, bar)` picks the LOWEST margin threshold whose accepted-set error stays within the duty's acceptance bar (accept the most locally ⇒ escalate the least). The split is DETERMINISTIC (index parity — no RNG, so a re-fit is byte-identical). Scope: **classification-shaped duties only** (the F7 boundary — margins presuppose a label token); generation duties (`utility`, `distill-summarize`) have no margin and are governed by their own S12.4 rows (recorded honestly, never a pseudo-margin). Uncalibrated ⇒ `AcceptLocal` returns true (the duty's ungated v0 behaviour continues — the `calibrated=false` VRAM-ledger precedent).
- **Calibration + battery records are platform DATA keyed (duty, model hash, engine build) (OQ1(a); migration 0010, R3/R9):** `calstore.go` (`*CalStore`) over `calibration_records` + `battery_results` — model hash = `SeatRecord.ModelHash()`, engine build = `LlamaCppPin`. Latest-wins upsert (a re-calibration/re-run REPLACES a key's row; not an event log). This is EXACTLY the key the S12.10 swap gate keeps valid (P-T15-1) and `EntailmentGate.Calibrated` reads. The **Def.8 result files in `P3/measurements/` remain the human-readable record — both, not either**. Regular rowid tables (the storage `.dump` backup orders by rowid — never `WITHOUT ROWID`).
- **The low-margin re-check is wired ONLY where a consumer exists (R4/R5):** `recheck.go` (`ReChecker`) — a below-threshold fast-tier `intake-triage` answer re-checks on the WORKHORSE seat ($0, local→local, via `Duty.CallSeat`); uncalibrated ⇒ no gate (nil-safe). The B5 consumers (`watchdog-disambiguator` → verdict IS `unclear`; watchlist re-check; intent-filling card) are SEAMS — their margin plumbing (`LabelMargin`) exists but no consumer is faked. `watchdog-disambiguator` NEVER escalates beyond `unclear` and NEVER gains a cloud fallback (recorded posture). Escalation-target posture is DATA on the verdict (billed to the requester's window when open, queued as parked work when empty — 3.9's boundary made concrete); NO paid-escalation wiring lands (OQ2 narrow).
- **The S12.10 swap gate is the SOLE `⚙ local.alias` write surface (R10–R12):** `swap.go` (`SwapGate.Retarget`). The **promotion rule** (`DecidePromotion`, R10 verbatim): a challenger replaces an incumbent only if it wins ≥2 duty suites with NON-overlapping 95% Wilson intervals (`WilsonInterval`, stdlib) at EQUAL-OR-BETTER tokens/s. The **P-T15-1 hard block** (R11): a retarget is REFUSED unless fresh calibration AND the affected battery suite exist for the NEW (duty, model hash, engine build) key — BEFORE the alias goes live (`ErrUncalibratedTarget`/`ErrBatterySuiteNotRun`, proven non-tautological by test). The ⚙ write flows through the `AliasWriter` seam (the shell's `aliasWriter` over `Registry.Set` — one tx: override + `settings_events` + `settings.changed`); `internal/local` never imports `settings`. Every swap fires the **7.3 revalidation trigger** (R12) through the `FlagByModelFunc` seam satisfied by `worker.Store.FlagByModel` at the root (`internal/local` never imports `worker`, §26 — the `memory.Committer` precedent; empty v0 worker set ⇒ flags nothing, documented honest).
- **The T15 battery is S12's acceptance instrument, versioned in-repo (R6–R9):** `internal/local/battery` (subpackage; imports `internal/local` + stdlib). Seed suites IN CODE + strict-JSON `LoadSuite`/`WriteSuite` overrides (the verify/intake `Load*` precedent); ~30–50 labeled cases per covered duty (synthetic bring-up seeds, grown from traces under S14/B5 governance — `WarnBelow` surfaces a thin suite). Classification suites are label-scored + produce `LabeledItem` margins (the calibration input reuses the runner, R25); generation suites are output-contract-scored (schema/length/grounding, no margin). Batch passes are **AC-only**: `Runner.CanRun` consumes ⚙ `local.batch.ac_only` (its FIRST consumer, R7) via a sleep-safe host power observation (`local.PowerReader`), refusing on battery. **`embedder` has no suite (post-gate, G2 Def.8); `sql-open` measures the alias, not the guardrail query surface (S14/B5).**
- **The KL quant sanity check (R8) — EXECUTED on the 4B default quant (drain C9 corrects round 1's false "executed on the two default-path quants"):** `kl.go` — `llama-perplexity --kl-divergence` over the ~250K-token Sinet-domain corpus (`corpus.go`, assembled deterministically from repo text: Spec/Research/Docs — platform-owned) against the BF16 baseline, two-step (baseline logits then compare), output parsed tolerantly. `llama-perplexity`/`llama-bench` are ADDITIONAL BUILD TARGETS of the pinned b10085 tree (same lock entry, no adoption — R32). Validates WEIGHTS quant only; KV stays fp16/q8_0. **BOTH default-path quants EXECUTED (drain round 2 completed the 9B leg; coordinator N1 fix — this clause's third state is the true final one): 4B Q4_K_M mean KLD 0.0369 / PPL ratio 1.017 / same-top-p 90.0%; 9B Q5_K_M mean KLD 0.0145 / PPL ratio 1.009 / same-top-p 94.0% — both faithful (`2026-07-22-kl-quant-check.md`).** The 9B BF16 baseline exceeds the 12.83 GB pool, so its base-dump ran CPU-side — a MEASURED ~13 min (5.70 s/pass probe), correcting round 1's unmeasured "multi-hour/infeasible" estimate. The harness binds any quant against its BF16 parent at the seat's promotion moment.
- **The two named seams go LIVE, wall-clean (R16/R17):** `memory.ContradictionScreen` — the adapter lives in `internal/shell` (NOT `internal/stage`: the §17 wall bars stage from importing `memory`), one-stage per OQ4(a) (workhorse CONFIRM via `CallSeat` — the DeBERTa alias target is `Servable:false` at the pin; the pre-screen re-enters when a serving path exists), high-precision (contradicts=true only on a confident "yes"), question-card-only, the deterministic same-topic_key detection NEVER suppressed, nil-safe. `verify.EntailmentChecker` — the adapter lives in `internal/stage` (stage already imports verify), the Guardian seat, binary + P(yes) from the verdict-token margin, LIVE-wired but the `EntailmentGate` stays IDLE (`Active` requires the web-research domain AND `Calibrated`). Both are advisory local calls and meter a $0 D7 row via the `AdvisoryMeter` seam (a short-lived platform run per run-less gate-write call — the §26 metering contract holds; a nil meter falls back to a fixed run id, surfacing the honest defect if absent).
- **The VRAM footprint key widens to the full S12.7 tuple (B4-6 F5 carry, R18):** `vram.go` `Ledger.footprints` is keyed by `FootprintKey{model, quant, context, slots, engine build}` (`SetFootprint`/`FootprintByKey`). The calibration run (R21) fills it; an unmeasured tuple stays honest-uncalibrated; admission continues to rest on the LIVE `memory.free` reading regardless.
